- **residenza-cambio**: Guide residence change with 45-day check and effects. Use when asked cambio residenza, move residence Italy, iscrizione anagrafica. ([terzastella/italy-work-skills](https://github.com/terzastella/italy-work-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/residenza-cambio) · ★2)
- **residual**: Inspect and drain the proactive-residual queue at .dynos/proactive-findings.json. Subcommands: list, run-next. Runs only when the user explicitly types /dynos-work:residual; never auto-triggered from conversation. ([dynos-fit/dynos-work](https://github.com/dynos-fit/dynos-work/tree/HEAD/skills/residual) · ★2)
- **residual-auth-state-preservation**: Preserve and verify authorization state that language agents must maintain under token revocation, session expiry, and delegated-scope changes, and keep stored user approvals bound to the context they were granted in. Derived from "ResidualAuth" (arXiv:2609.08062); approval replay evidence from "When Consent Outlives Context" (arXiv:2609.33910). ([Marontis/agentic-engineering](https://github.com/Marontis/agentic-engineering/tree/HEAD/skills/residual-auth-state-preservation) · ★3)
- **residual-based-covariate-adjustment**: Use when a pathway-level kernel association test (PaIRKAT) must be run on metabolomics data that are confounded by clinical covariates, so covariate effects must be removed from each metabolite before computing kernel quadratic-form statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/residual-based-covariate-adjustment))
- **residual-based-peak-parameter-initialization**: Use when initializing peak parameters for SAND's per-bin time-domain decomposition of 1D NMR spectra so the hybrid optimizer starts from data-driven seeds rather than arbitrary guesses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/residual-based-peak-parameter-initialization))
- **residual-conservative-mpc**: Residual-Conservative MPC (RC-MPPI) framework — adaptive safety modulation for sampling-based model predictive control using prediction-execution residuals. Combines residual-dependent constraint tightening, adaptive safety-cost shaping, and residual-adaptive sampling. Use when designing safety-critical MPC systems with model uncertainty. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/residual-conservative-mpc) · ★4)
- **residual-convolution-layer-implementation**: Use when reconstructing or re-implementing Chiron's segmentation-free basecalling network in TensorFlow, specifically when building the CNN portion that converts raw .fast5 electrical signal into features for the bidirectional LSTM layers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/residual-convolution-layer-implementation))
- **residual-diagnosis**: The mandatory discipline BEFORE chasing any fidelity residual (an F_diff-vs-C gap, an S-panel miss, an energy/closure discrepancy) — state the reference basis and a falsifiable hypothesis, confirm the comparison basis is correct, and time-box before writing probe scripts. Use it at the start of any "why doesn't X match Y?" investigation. ALSO how to trisect the fallout when a basis error IS found (ADR 0060): a RATIO over time is partly robust to a basis substitution while a LEVEL is not, so label every downstream claim ratio-or-level before re-measuring; emit both columns side by side rather… ([rimajj/LPJmLFIT_Emulator](https://github.com/rimajj/LPJmLFIT_Emulator/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/residual-diagnosis) · ★1)
- **residual-discordance-fraction-derivation**: Use when comparing binary molecular subgroups (e.g., phosphoAKT1-high or MYC-high from k-means clustering) against SNP-array alteration calls (10q/PTEN loss, 8q/MYC gain) and you need the fraction of altered cases within each subgroup denominator.
